Introductiones Apotelesmaticae (Johannes ab Indagine, 1522)
==========================================================
Indagine's 1522 elegant introductions to chiromancy, physiognomy and astrology; the Renaissance divination encyclopedia. Public domain. Digitized from Internet Archive (https://archive.org/details/hin-wel-all-00001272-001).
